#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Scenario: Second C on same piece is blocked (hold-3 / UX Intent: Interaction Feel)
# Surface: TUI / terminal (tmux + capture-pane)
# Precondition: Game in playing phase, hold already used this piece.
# Expected: Pressing C a second time produces no visible change — HOLD panel stays the same.

set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
PROJECT_ROOT="$(cd "$SCRIPT_DIR/../.." && pwd)"
TETRIS_BIN="$PROJECT_ROOT/tetris"
SESSION="tetris-hold2"

command tmux kill-session -t "$SESSION" 2>/dev/null || true
command tmux new-session -d -s "$SESSION" -x 80 -y 30
command tmux send-keys -t "$SESSION" "$TETRIS_BIN" Enter
sleep 2.5

# Start game.
command tmux send-keys -lt "$SESSION" $'\r'
sleep 0.5

# First C — hold the active piece.
command tmux send-keys -lt "$SESSION" "c"
sleep 0.3

AFTER_FIRST=$(command tmux capture-pane -t "$SESSION" -p)

# Second C — must be blocked.
command tmux send-keys -lt "$SESSION" "c"
sleep 0.3

AFTER_SECOND=$(command tmux capture-pane -t "$SESSION" -p)

command tmux kill-session -t "$SESSION" 2>/dev/null || true

# Compare HOLD panel content between first and second press.
# The HOLD panel occupies 6 rows starting with "HOLD"; extract only those lines
# from the left column (up to the first │ ... │ that is the hold panel, not board).
# Strategy: grep for lines that contain "HOLD" label or hold-panel-width bordered rows.
# We compare the left-side 8-char panel only, ignoring board content on the same line.
extract_hold_panel() {
  echo "$1" | awk '
    /HOLD/ { found=1 }
    found { print substr($0, 1, 20); count++ }
    count >= 6 { exit }
  '
}

HOLD_AFTER_FIRST=$(extract_hold_panel "$AFTER_FIRST")
HOLD_AFTER_SECOND=$(extract_hold_panel "$AFTER_SECOND")

if [ "$HOLD_AFTER_FIRST" != "$HOLD_AFTER_SECOND" ]; then
  echo "FAIL: HOLD panel changed after second C press — hold should be blocked" >&2
  echo "--- After first C (hold panel only) ---" >&2
  echo "$HOLD_AFTER_FIRST" >&2
  echo "--- After second C (hold panel only) ---" >&2
  echo "$HOLD_AFTER_SECOND" >&2
  exit 1
fi

echo "PASS: second C press on same piece is blocked — HOLD panel unchanged"
```

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Scenario: After new spawn, hold dim clears (hold-1 state transitions / UX Intent: State Transitions)
# Surface: TUI / terminal (tmux + capture-pane)
# Precondition: Hold was used on previous piece; hard drop spawns new piece.
# Expected: After hard drop the dim treatment is gone — hold is re-available for the new piece.
#   Pressing C after new spawn is accepted (hold works again).

set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
PROJECT_ROOT="$(cd "$SCRIPT_DIR/../.." && pwd)"
TETRIS_BIN="$PROJECT_ROOT/tetris"
SESSION="tetris-hold3"

command tmux kill-session -t "$SESSION" 2>/dev/null || true
command tmux new-session -d -s "$SESSION" -x 80 -y 30
command tmux send-keys -t "$SESSION" "$TETRIS_BIN" Enter
sleep 2.5

# Start game.
command tmux send-keys -lt "$SESSION" $'\r'
sleep 0.5

# Hold the first piece.
command tmux send-keys -lt "$SESSION" "c"
sleep 0.3

AFTER_HOLD=$(command tmux capture-pane -t "$SESSION" -p)

# Hard drop to spawn a new piece (resets hold_used).
command tmux send-keys -lt "$SESSION" " "
sleep 0.5

AFTER_SPAWN=$(command tmux capture-pane -t "$SESSION" -p)

# Hold again — should succeed (new piece, hold_used = false).
command tmux send-keys -lt "$SESSION" "c"
sleep 0.3

AFTER_SECOND_HOLD=$(command tmux capture-pane -t "$SESSION" -p)

command tmux kill-session -t "$SESSION" 2>/dev/null || true

# 1. After hard drop: HOLD panel must still show a piece (held piece persists).
if ! echo "$AFTER_SPAWN" | grep -A5 "HOLD" | grep -q "██"; then
  echo "FAIL: HOLD panel has no ██ after spawn — held piece should persist" >&2
  echo "--- AFTER_SPAWN ---" >&2
  echo "$AFTER_SPAWN" >&2
  exit 1
fi

# 2. After second hold: HOLD panel must update (swap happened — new active piece was held).
HOLD_AFTER_FIRST=$(echo "$AFTER_HOLD" | grep -A5 "HOLD")
HOLD_AFTER_SECOND_HOLD=$(echo "$AFTER_SECOND_HOLD" | grep -A5 "HOLD")

# The panel must still contain block glyphs (held piece present).
if ! echo "$AFTER_SECOND_HOLD" | grep -A5 "HOLD" | grep -q "██"; then
  echo "FAIL: HOLD panel missing ██ after second hold" >&2
  echo "--- AFTER_SECOND_HOLD ---" >&2
  echo "$AFTER_SECOND_HOLD" >&2
  exit 1
fi

# 3. Required panel labels must still be present.
for elem in "HOLD" "NEXT" "SCO" "LVL" "LNS"; do
  if ! echo "$AFTER_SECOND_HOLD" | grep -q "$elem"; then
    echo "FAIL: frame element '$elem' missing after second hold" >&2
    exit 1
  fi
done

echo "PASS: hold dim clears after new spawn; second hold on new piece is accepted"
```

```bash
extract_hold_panel() {
  python3 -c "
import sys
lines = sys.stdin.read().splitlines()
in_panel = False
count = 0
for line in lines:
    if '│ HOLD │' in line or (in_panel and count < 5):
        if not in_panel:
            in_panel = True
        # Extract chars 20-28 (hold panel columns)
        padded = line.ljust(28)
        print(padded[20:28])
        count += 1
        if count >= 6:
            break
" <<< "\$1"
}
```

  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Scenario: Hold with empty slot (QA-HOLD-1)\n# Surface: TUI / terminal (tmux + capture-pane)\n# Precondition: Game in playing phase, hold slot empty.\n# Expected:\n#   - Before C: HOLD panel interior is blank (no ██ or ░░).\n#   - After C: HOLD panel shows ██ glyphs (held piece, dim treatment).\n#   - Board shows a new active piece (new piece spawned).\n#   - Score is unchanged (holding does not score).\n#   - Both updates appear in the same frame.\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nPROJECT_ROOT=\"$(cd \"$SCRIPT_DIR/../..\" && pwd)\"\nTETRIS_BIN=\"$PROJECT_ROOT/tetris\"\nSESSION=\"tetris-hold1\"\n\nextract_hold_panel() {\n  # Extract the 8 characters of the hold panel (cols 20-28) for 6 lines starting at top border.\n  python3 -c \"\nimport sys\nlines = sys.stdin.read().splitlines()\nfor i, line in enumerate(lines):\n    if '│ HOLD │' in line:\n        start = max(0, i - 1)\n        for j in range(start, start + 7):\n            if j < len(lines):\n                padded = lines[j] + ' ' * 30\n                print(padded[20:28])\n        break\n\"\n}\n\nextract_score() {\n  # Extract score value from the line after the SCO label.\n  python3 -c \"\nimport sys\nlines = sys.stdin.read().splitlines()\nfor i, line in enumerate(lines):\n    if 'SCO' in line and i + 1 < len(lines):\n        padded = lines[i + 1] + ' ' * 30\n        print(padded[52:58].strip())\n        break\n\"\n}\n\ncommand tmux kill-session -t \"$SESSION\" 2>/dev/null || true\ncommand tmux new-session -d -s \"$SESSION\" -x 80 -y 30\ncommand tmux send-keys -t \"$SESSION\" \"$TETRIS_BIN\" Enter\nsleep 2.5\n\n# Start game.\ncommand tmux send-keys -lt \"$SESSION\" $'\\r'\nsleep 0.5\n\nBEFORE=$(command tmux capture-pane -t \"$SESSION\" -p)\n\nSCORE_BEFORE=$(extract_score <<< \"$BEFORE\")\nHOLD_BEFORE=$(extract_hold_panel <<< \"$BEFORE\")\n\n# 1. Before hold: HOLD panel interior must have no block glyphs.\nif echo \"$HOLD_BEFORE\" | grep -q \"██\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL: HOLD panel has ██ glyphs before hold — should be blank\" >&2\n  echo \"--- Hold panel before hold ---\" >&2\n  echo \"$HOLD_BEFORE\" >&2\n  command tmux kill-session -t \"$SESSION\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# Press C to hold.\ncommand tmux send-keys -lt \"$SESSION\" \"c\"\nsleep 0.3\n\nAFTER=$(command tmux capture-pane -t \"$SESSION\" -p)\n\ncommand tmux kill-session -t \"$SESSION\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\nHOLD_AFTER=$(extract_hold_panel <<< \"$AFTER\")\nSCORE_AFTER=$(extract_score <<< \"$AFTER\")\n\n# 2. After hold: HOLD panel must contain ██ glyphs.\nif ! echo \"$HOLD_AFTER\" | grep -q \"██\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL: HOLD panel does not show ██ glyphs after hold\" >&2\n  echo \"--- HOLD panel after hold ---\" >&2\n  echo \"$HOLD_AFTER\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# 3. Score must not change when holding.\nif [ \"$SCORE_BEFORE\" != \"$SCORE_AFTER\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL: score changed from '$SCORE_BEFORE' to '$SCORE_AFTER' — hold must not score\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# 4. Frame must have all required panel labels.\nfor elem in \"HOLD\" \"NEXT\" \"SCO\" \"LVL\" \"LNS\"; do\n  if ! echo \"$AFTER\" | grep -q \"$elem\"; then\n    echo \"FAIL: frame element '$elem' missing after hold\" >&2\n    exit 1\n  fi\ndone\n\n# 5. Board must have an active piece (at least one ██ cell in the board area).\nBOARD_ACTIVE=$(echo \"$AFTER\" | python3 -c \"\nimport sys\nlines = sys.stdin.read().splitlines()\nfor line in lines:\n    padded = line + ' ' * 60\n    board = padded[29:51]  # board occupies cols 29-50 (22 wide = 11 cells * 2)\n    if '██' in board:\n        print('yes')\n        break\n\")\nif [ \"$BOARD_ACTIVE\" != \"yes\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL: no ██ glyphs in board area after hold — active piece missing\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\necho \"PASS: hold with empty slot — panel fills with held piece, score unchanged, board shows new active piece\"\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/gabadi/workspace/gamaleds/tetris/.worktrees/QA/observation-harness/hold/hold2_second_press_blocked.sh",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Scenario: Second C on same piece is blocked (QA-HOLD-3)\n# Surface: TUI / terminal (tmux + capture-pane)\n# Precondition: Game in playing phase, hold already used this piece.\n# Expected:\n#   - Pressing C a second time produces no visible change — HOLD panel stays the same.\n#   - Score is unchanged.\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nPROJECT_ROOT=\"$(cd \"$SCRIPT_DIR/../..\" && pwd)\"\nTETRIS_BIN=\"$PROJECT_ROOT/tetris\"\nSESSION=\"tetris-hold2\"\n\nextract_hold_panel() {\n  python3 -c \"\nimport sys\nlines = sys.stdin.read().splitlines()\nfor i, line in enumerate(lines):\n    if '│ HOLD │' in line:\n        start = max(0, i - 1)\n        for j in range(start, start + 7):\n            if j < len(lines):\n                padded = lines[j] + ' ' * 30\n                print(padded[20:28])\n        break\n\"\n}\n\nextract_score() {\n  python3 -c \"\nimport sys\nlines = sys.stdin.read().splitlines()\nfor i, line in enumerate(lines):\n    if 'SCO' in line and i + 1 < len(lines):\n        padded = lines[i + 1] + ' ' * 30\n        print(padded[53:59])\n        break\n\"\n}\n\ncommand tmux kill-session -t \"$SESSION\" 2>/dev/null || true\ncommand tmux new-session -d -s \"$SESSION\" -x 80 -y 30\ncommand tmux send-keys -t \"$SESSION\" \"$TETRIS_BIN\" Enter\nsleep 2.5\n\n# Start game.\ncommand tmux send-keys -lt \"$SESSION\" $'\\r'\nsleep 0.5\n\n# First C — hold the active piece.\ncommand tmux send-keys -lt \"$SESSION\" \"c\"\nsleep 0.3\n\nAFTER_FIRST=$(command tmux capture-pane -t \"$SESSION\" -p)\n\nHOLD_AFTER_FIRST=$(extract_hold_panel <<< \"$AFTER_FIRST\")\nSCORE_AFTER_FIRST=$(extract_score <<< \"$AFTER_FIRST\")\n\n# Second C — must be blocked.\ncommand tmux send-keys -lt \"$SESSION\" \"c\"\nsleep 0.3\n\nAFTER_SECOND=$(command tmux capture-pane -t \"$SESSION\" -p)\n\ncommand tmux kill-session -t \"$SESSION\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\nHOLD_AFTER_SECOND=$(extract_hold_panel <<< \"$AFTER_SECOND\")\nSCORE_AFTER_SECOND=$(extract_score <<< \"$AFTER_SECOND\")\n\n# 1. HOLD panel must not change after second C press.\nif [ \"$HOLD_AFTER_FIRST\" != \"$HOLD_AFTER_SECOND\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL: HOLD panel changed after second C press — hold should be blocked\" >&2\n  echo \"--- Hold panel after first C ---\" >&2\n  echo \"$HOLD_AFTER_FIRST\" >&2\n  echo \"--- Hold panel after second C ---\" >&2\n  echo \"$HOLD_AFTER_SECOND\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# 2. Score must not change on second (blocked) hold.\nif [ \"$SCORE_AFTER_FIRST\" != \"$SCORE_AFTER_SECOND\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL: score changed from '$SCORE_AFTER_FIRST' to '$SCORE_AFTER_SECOND' — blocked hold must not score\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\necho \"PASS: second C press on same piece is blocked — HOLD panel and score unchanged\"\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/gabadi/workspace/gamaleds/tetris/.worktrees/QA/observation-harness/hold/hold3_swap_on_spawn.sh",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Scenario: After new spawn, hold dim clears (QA-HOLD-4)\n# Surface: TUI / terminal (tmux + capture-pane)\n# Precondition: Hold was used on previous piece; hard drop spawns new piece.\n# Expected:\n#   - After hard drop: held piece still shows in HOLD panel.\n#   - After second C (swap): HOLD panel updates with new held piece.\n#   - Score is unchanged (holding does not score).\n#   - Pressing C after new spawn is accepted (hold works again).\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nPROJECT_ROOT=\"$(cd \"$SCRIPT_DIR/../..\" && pwd)\"\nTETRIS_BIN=\"$PROJECT_ROOT/tetris\"\nSESSION=\"tetris-hold3\"\n\nextract_hold_panel() {\n  python3 -c \"\nimport sys\nlines = sys.stdin.read().splitlines()\nfor i, line in enumerate(lines):\n    if '│ HOLD │' in line:\n        start = max(0, i - 1)\n        for j in range(start, start + 7):\n            if j < len(lines):\n                padded = lines[j] + ' ' * 30\n                print(padded[20:28])\n        break\n\"\n}\n\nextract_score() {\n  python3 -c \"\nimport sys\nlines = sys.stdin.read().splitlines()\nfor i, line in enumerate(lines):\n    if 'SCO' in line and i + 1 < len(lines):\n        padded = lines[i + 1] + ' ' * 30\n        print(padded[53:59])\n        break\n\"\n}\n\nhold_panel_has_piece() {\n  extract_hold_panel <<< \"$1\" | grep -q \"██\"\n}\n\ncommand tmux kill-session -t \"$SESSION\" 2>/dev/null || true\ncommand tmux new-session -d -s \"$SESSION\" -x 80 -y 30\ncommand tmux send-keys -t \"$SESSION\" \"$TETRIS_BIN\" Enter\nsleep 2.5\n\n# Start game.\ncommand tmux send-keys -lt \"$SESSION\" $'\\r'\nsleep 0.5\n\nBEFORE_HOLD=$(command tmux capture-pane -t \"$SESSION\" -p)\nSCORE_BEFORE=$(extract_score <<< \"$BEFORE_HOLD\")\n\n# Hold the first piece.\ncommand tmux send-keys -lt \"$SESSION\" \"c\"\nsleep 0.3\n\nAFTER_HOLD=$(command tmux capture-pane -t \"$SESSION\" -p)\n\n# Verify hold panel has piece after first hold.\nif ! hold_panel_has_piece \"$AFTER_HOLD\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL: HOLD panel has no ██ after first hold\" >&2\n  echo \"--- Hold panel ---\" >&2\n  extract_hold_panel <<< \"$AFTER_HOLD\" >&2\n  command tmux kill-session -t \"$SESSION\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# Hard drop to spawn a new piece (resets hold_used).\ncommand tmux send-keys -lt \"$SESSION\" \" \"\nsleep 0.5\n\nAFTER_SPAWN=$(command tmux capture-pane -t \"$SESSION\" -p)\n\n# 1. After hard drop: HOLD panel must still show a piece (held piece persists).\nif ! hold_panel_has_piece \"$AFTER_SPAWN\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL: HOLD panel lost piece after hard drop spawn\" >&2\n  echo \"--- Hold panel after spawn ---\" >&2\n  extract_hold_panel <<< \"$AFTER_SPAWN\" >&2\n  command tmux kill-session -t \"$SESSION\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# Hold again — should succeed (new piece, hold_used = false).\ncommand tmux send-keys -lt \"$SESSION\" \"c\"\nsleep 0.3\n\nAFTER_SECOND_HOLD=$(command tmux capture-pane -t \"$SESSION\" -p)\n\ncommand tmux kill-session -t \"$SESSION\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\n# 2. After second hold: HOLD panel must still have a piece.\nif ! hold_panel_has_piece \"$AFTER_SECOND_HOLD\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL: HOLD panel has no ██ after second hold (swap)\" >&2\n  echo \"--- Hold panel after second hold ---\" >&2\n  extract_hold_panel <<< \"$AFTER_SECOND_HOLD\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# 3. Score must be unchanged (holding does not score).\nSCORE_AFTER=$(extract_score <<< \"$AFTER_SECOND_HOLD\")\nif [ \"$SCORE_BEFORE\" != \"$SCORE_AFTER\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL: score changed from '$SCORE_BEFORE' to '$SCORE_AFTER' — hold must not score\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# 4. Required panel labels must still be present.\nfor elem in \"HOLD\" \"NEXT\" \"SCO\" \"LVL\" \"LNS\"; do\n  if ! echo \"$AFTER_SECOND_HOLD\" | grep -q \"$elem\"; then\n    echo \"FAIL: frame element '$elem' missing after second hold\" >&2\n    exit 1\n  fi\ndone\n\necho \"PASS: hold dim clears after new spawn; second hold on new piece is accepted; score unchanged\"\n"
}

  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Scenario: Hold swap — held piece becomes active, active becomes held (QA-HOLD-2)\n# Surface: TUI / terminal (tmux + capture-pane)\n# Precondition: Game playing, hold slot occupied (from prior hold), hold_used reset after spawn.\n# Expected:\n#   - After first C: hold panel fills (hold used, dim).\n#   - After hard drop + new spawn: hold panel still has piece; hold available.\n#   - After second C (swap): hold panel updates (different kind now held), active piece present.\n#   - Score is unchanged throughout (holding does not score).\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nPROJECT_ROOT=\"$(cd \"$SCRIPT_DIR/../..\" && pwd)\"\nTETRIS_BIN=\"$PROJECT_ROOT/tetris\"\nSESSION=\"tetris-hold4\"\n\nextract_hold_panel() {\n  python3 -c \"\nimport sys\nlines = sys.stdin.read().splitlines()\nfor i, line in enumerate(lines):\n    if '│ HOLD │' in line:\n        start = max(0, i - 1)\n        for j in range(start, start + 7):\n            if j < len(lines):\n                padded = lines[j] + ' ' * 30\n                print(padded[20:28])\n        break\n\"\n}\n\nextract_score() {\n  python3 -c \"\nimport sys\nlines = sys.stdin.read().splitlines()\nfor i, line in enumerate(lines):\n    if 'SCO' in line and i + 1 < len(lines):\n        padded = lines[i + 1] + ' ' * 30\n        print(padded[53:59])\n        break\n\"\n}\n\nhold_panel_has_piece() {\n  extract_hold_panel <<< \"$1\" | grep -q \"██\"\n}\n\ncommand tmux kill-session -t \"$SESSION\" 2>/dev/null || true\ncommand tmux new-session -d -s \"$SESSION\" -x 80 -y 30\ncommand tmux send-keys -t \"$SESSION\" \"$TETRIS_BIN\" Enter\nsleep 2.5\n\n# Start game.\ncommand tmux send-keys -lt \"$SESSION\" $'\\r'\nsleep 0.5\n\nBEFORE=$(command tmux capture-pane -t \"$SESSION\" -p)\nSCORE_BEFORE=$(extract_score <<< \"$BEFORE\")\n\n# First C — store active piece in empty hold slot.\ncommand tmux send-keys -lt \"$SESSION\" \"c\"\nsleep 0.3\n\nAFTER_FIRST_HOLD=$(command tmux capture-pane -t \"$SESSION\" -p)\n\n# Hold panel must show a piece after first hold.\nif ! hold_panel_has_piece \"$AFTER_FIRST_HOLD\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL: hold panel has no ██ after first hold\" >&2\n  echo \"--- Hold panel ---\" >&2\n  extract_hold_panel <<< \"$AFTER_FIRST_HOLD\" >&2\n  command tmux kill-session -t \"$SESSION\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# Hard drop — new piece spawns, hold_used resets.\ncommand tmux send-keys -lt \"$SESSION\" \" \"\nsleep 0.5\n\nAFTER_SPAWN=$(command tmux capture-pane -t \"$SESSION\" -p)\n\n# Hold panel must still have a piece after spawn.\nif ! hold_panel_has_piece \"$AFTER_SPAWN\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL: hold panel lost piece after hard drop spawn\" >&2\n  echo \"--- Hold panel after spawn ---\" >&2\n  extract_hold_panel <<< \"$AFTER_SPAWN\" >&2\n  command tmux kill-session -t \"$SESSION\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  exit 1\nfi\n\nHOLD_BEFORE_SWAP=$(extract_hold_panel <<< \"$AFTER_SPAWN\")\n\n# Second C — swap: held → active, active → held.\ncommand tmux send-keys -lt \"$SESSION\" \"c\"\nsleep 0.3\n\nAFTER_SWAP=$(command tmux capture-pane -t \"$SESSION\" -p)\n\ncommand tmux kill-session -t \"$SESSION\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\nHOLD_AFTER_SWAP=$(extract_hold_panel <<< \"$AFTER_SWAP\")\n\n# 1. After swap: hold panel must still show a piece (new held kind).\nif ! hold_panel_has_piece \"$AFTER_SWAP\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL: hold panel has no ██ after swap\" >&2\n  echo \"--- Hold panel after swap ---\" >&2\n  echo \"$HOLD_AFTER_SWAP\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# 2. Active piece must still be present on board.\nBOARD_ACTIVE=$(echo \"$AFTER_SWAP\" | python3 -c \"\nimport sys\nlines = sys.stdin.read().splitlines()\nfor line in lines:\n    padded = line + ' ' * 60\n    board = padded[31:51]\n    if '██' in board:\n        print('yes')\n        break\n\")\nif [ \"$BOARD_ACTIVE\" != \"yes\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL: no active piece on board after swap\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# 3. Score must be unchanged (holding does not score).\nSCORE_AFTER=$(extract_score <<< \"$AFTER_SWAP\")\nif [ \"$SCORE_BEFORE\" != \"$SCORE_AFTER\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL: score changed from '$SCORE_BEFORE' to '$SCORE_AFTER' — hold must not score\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# 4. Required panel labels must be present.\nfor elem in \"HOLD\" \"NEXT\" \"SCO\" \"LVL\" \"LNS\"; do\n  if ! echo \"$AFTER_SWAP\" | grep -q \"$elem\"; then\n    echo \"FAIL: frame element '$elem' missing after swap\" >&2\n    exit 1\n  fi\ndone\n\necho \"PASS: hold swap — held piece becomes active, active piece becomes held, score unchanged\"\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/gabadi/workspace/gamaleds/tetris/.worktrees/QA/observation-harness/hold/hold5_paused_ignored.sh",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Scenario: Hold ignored when paused (QA-HOLD-5)\n# Surface: TUI / terminal (tmux + capture-pane)\n# Precondition: Game playing, hold slot empty.\n# Expected:\n#   - After pause (P): HOLD panel visible, hold slot empty.\n#   - While paused, pressing C: HOLD panel content unchanged (no piece stored).\n#   - After resume (P): hold slot still empty (no hold occurred).\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nPROJECT_ROOT=\"$(cd \"$SCRIPT_DIR/../..\" && pwd)\"\nTETRIS_BIN=\"$PROJECT_ROOT/tetris\"\nSESSION=\"tetris-hold5\"\n\nextract_hold_panel() {\n  python3 -c \"\nimport sys\nlines = sys.stdin.read().splitlines()\nfor i, line in enumerate(lines):\n    if '│ HOLD │' in line:\n        start = max(0, i - 1)\n        for j in range(start, start + 7):\n            if j < len(lines):\n                padded = lines[j] + ' ' * 30\n                print(padded[20:28])\n        break\n\"\n}\n\ncommand tmux kill-session -t \"$SESSION\" 2>/dev/null || true\ncommand tmux new-session -d -s \"$SESSION\" -x 80 -y 30\ncommand tmux send-keys -t \"$SESSION\" \"$TETRIS_BIN\" Enter\nsleep 2.5\n\n# Start game.\ncommand tmux send-keys -lt \"$SESSION\" $'\\r'\nsleep 0.5\n\n# Pause.\ncommand tmux send-keys -lt \"$SESSION\" \"p\"\nsleep 0.3\n\nAFTER_PAUSE=$(command tmux capture-pane -t \"$SESSION\" -p)\n\n# Verify paused — PAUSED text must appear.\nif ! echo \"$AFTER_PAUSE\" | grep -qi \"PAUSED\\|paused\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL: game did not pause — PAUSED not visible\" >&2\n  echo \"--- AFTER_PAUSE ---\" >&2\n  echo \"$AFTER_PAUSE\" >&2\n  command tmux kill-session -t \"$SESSION\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# Hold panel must be visible while paused.\nif ! echo \"$AFTER_PAUSE\" | grep -q \"HOLD\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL: HOLD panel not visible while paused\" >&2\n  command tmux kill-session -t \"$SESSION\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  exit 1\nfi\n\nHOLD_BEFORE_C=$(extract_hold_panel <<< \"$AFTER_PAUSE\")\n\n# Press C while paused — must be ignored.\ncommand tmux send-keys -lt \"$SESSION\" \"c\"\nsleep 0.3\n\nAFTER_C_PAUSED=$(command tmux capture-pane -t \"$SESSION\" -p)\nHOLD_AFTER_C=$(extract_hold_panel <<< \"$AFTER_C_PAUSED\")\n\nif [ \"$HOLD_BEFORE_C\" != \"$HOLD_AFTER_C\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL: HOLD panel changed while paused — C should be ignored\" >&2\n  echo \"--- Hold panel before C ---\" >&2\n  echo \"$HOLD_BEFORE_C\" >&2\n  echo \"--- Hold panel after C ---\" >&2\n  echo \"$HOLD_AFTER_C\" >&2\n  command tmux kill-session -t \"$SESSION\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# Resume.\ncommand tmux send-keys -lt \"$SESSION\" \"p\"\nsleep 0.3\n\nAFTER_RESUME=$(command tmux capture-pane -t \"$SESSION\" -p)\nHOLD_AFTER_RESUME=$(extract_hold_panel <<< \"$AFTER_RESUME\")\n\ncommand tmux kill-session -t \"$SESSION\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\n# Hold panel must be unchanged after resume (hold was not executed while paused).\nif [ \"$HOLD_BEFORE_C\" != \"$HOLD_AFTER_RESUME\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL: HOLD panel changed after resume — hold should have been ignored while paused\" >&2\n  echo \"--- Hold panel before C (paused) ---\" >&2\n  echo \"$HOLD_BEFORE_C\" >&2\n  echo \"--- Hold panel after resume ---\" >&2\n  echo \"$HOLD_AFTER_RESUME\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\necho \"PASS: hold C ignored while paused — hold panel unchanged before, during, and after pause\"\n"
}
```

Now write hold7 properly — automatable via `--seed 42` + 2x CW rotate + hold, then assert J-north pattern:

The J piece north normalized cells: `{0,0},{0,1},{1,1},{2,1}`
With `previewCenterOffset`: maxCol=2 → offset = (3-3)/2 = 0
- previewRow 0: col 0 → `[0]=true, [1]=false, [2]=false` → `██    ` (1 glyph)
- previewRow 1: cols 0,1,2 → `[0][1][2]=true` → `██████` (3 glyphs)
- previewRow 2: nothing → `      ` (empty)

J piece south normalized cells: `{0,1},{1,1},{2,1},{2,2}`
With offset: maxCol=2 → offset=0
- previewRow 0: nothing → `      ` (empty)
- previewRow 1: cols 0,1,2 → `██████`
- previewRow 2: col 2 → `    ██`

So the distinguishing assertion: previewRow 0 (hold panel row 2) should have `██` for north, empty for south.

With seed 42, first piece is J. After 2x CW rotation (→ south), we hold. The hold panel must show north (row 0 has `██`), not south (row 0 is empty).

{
  "file_path": "/Users/gabadi/workspace/gamaleds/tetris/.worktrees/QA/observation-harness/hold/hold7_north_rotation_preview.sh",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Scenario: Hold preview renders in north rotation (QA-HOLD-7)\n# Surface: TUI / terminal (tmux + capture-pane)\n# Precondition: seed=42 (first piece = J), rotate 2x CW (south rotation), then hold.\n# Expected: Hold panel shows J in north rotation — previewRow 0 has ██ (J-north top cell),\n#   previewRow 1 has ██████ (J-north full bottom row), previewRow 2 is empty.\n#   NOT the south rotation pattern (which would have previewRow 0 empty).\n#\n# Technique: With known seed, we know the exact piece kind and its north vs south footprint.\n# By rotating to south before holding, we verify that the hold panel resets to north.\n# J-north: row0={col0}, row1={col0,col1,col2}  →  previewRow0 has ██ at left\n# J-south: row0=empty, row1={col0,col1,col2}   →  previewRow0 is blank\n# These are distinguishable via capture-pane character content.\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nPROJECT_ROOT=\"$(cd \"$SCRIPT_DIR/../..\" && pwd)\"\nTETRIS_BIN=\"$PROJECT_ROOT/tetris\"\nSESSION=\"tetris-hold7\"\n\ncommand tmux kill-session -t \"$SESSION\" 2>/dev/null || true\ncommand tmux new-session -d -s \"$SESSION\" -x 80 -y 30\n# Use seed 42: first piece is J (confirmed via game.NewSession(rand.NewSource(42)))\ncommand tmux send-keys -t \"$SESSION\" \"$TETRIS_BIN --seed 42\" Enter\nsleep 2.5\n\n# Start game.\ncommand tmux send-keys -lt \"$SESSION\" $'\\r'\nsleep 0.5\n\n# Rotate 2x CW: J north → east → south.\ncommand tmux send-keys -lt \"$SESSION\" \"x\"\nsleep 0.1\ncommand tmux send-keys -lt \"$SESSION\" \"x\"\nsleep 0.1\n\n# Hold — piece is in south rotation; hold panel must reset to north.\ncommand tmux send-keys -lt \"$SESSION\" \"c\"\nsleep 0.3\n\nAFTER_HOLD=$(command tmux capture-pane -t \"$SESSION\" -p)\n\ncommand tmux kill-session -t \"$SESSION\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\n# Extract hold panel rows 2-4 (previewRows 0-2).\nHOLD_PREVIEW=$(python3 -c \"\nimport sys\nlines = sys.stdin.read().splitlines()\nfor i, line in enumerate(lines):\n    if '│ HOLD │' in line:\n        # preview rows are at offsets +1, +2, +3 from HOLD label row\n        for offset in range(1, 4):\n            j = i + offset\n            if j < len(lines):\n                padded = lines[j] + ' ' * 30\n                print(padded[20:28])\n        break\n\" <<< \"$AFTER_HOLD\")\n\nif [ -z \"$HOLD_PREVIEW\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL: could not extract hold preview rows\" >&2\n  echo \"--- Full frame ---\" >&2\n  echo \"$AFTER_HOLD\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# Parse the 3 preview rows.\nPREVIEW_ROW0=$(echo \"$HOLD_PREVIEW\" | sed -n '1p')\nPREVIEW_ROW1=$(echo \"$HOLD_PREVIEW\" | sed -n '2p')\nPREVIEW_ROW2=$(echo \"$HOLD_PREVIEW\" | sed -n '3p')\n\n# J-north assertion: previewRow 0 must have ██ (top-left cell of J).\nif ! echo \"$PREVIEW_ROW0\" | grep -q \"██\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL: hold panel previewRow 0 has no ██ — expected J-north (top-left cell)\" >&2\n  echo \"  previewRow 0: [$PREVIEW_ROW0]\" >&2\n  echo \"  previewRow 1: [$PREVIEW_ROW1]\" >&2\n  echo \"  previewRow 2: [$PREVIEW_ROW2]\" >&2\n  echo \"  (J-south would show empty row 0; this means north rotation was NOT used)\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# J-north assertion: previewRow 1 must have ██ at all 3 positions (full bottom row).\nif ! echo \"$PREVIEW_ROW1\" | grep -q \"██████\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL: hold panel previewRow 1 does not show full ██████ row — expected J-north bottom\" >&2\n  echo \"  previewRow 1: [$PREVIEW_ROW1]\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# J-north assertion: previewRow 2 must be empty (J is 2 rows tall in north).\nif echo \"$PREVIEW_ROW2\" | grep -q \"██\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL: hold panel previewRow 2 has ██ — J-north has no cells in row 2\" >&2\n  echo \"  previewRow 2: [$PREVIEW_ROW2]\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\necho \"PASS: hold panel shows J in north rotation (seed=42, rotated 2xCW to south before hold)\"\necho \"  previewRow 0 (has ██): [$PREVIEW_ROW0]\"\necho \"  previewRow 1 (full):   [$PREVIEW_ROW1]\"\necho \"  previewRow 2 (empty):  [$PREVIEW_ROW2]\"\n"
}

## QA-HOLD-4: Hold becomes available again after the next piece spawns

**Precondition:** Game is playing. Hold has been used on the current piece (Hold panel is dim).

**Steps:**
1. Let the current piece lock (drop it with `Space`, or wait for it to lock), so a new piece spawns.
2. Observe the Hold panel.
3. Press `C` once.

**Expected:**
- After the new piece spawns, the Hold panel piece returns to full color (no longer dim) — hold
  is available again.
- Pressing `C` is now accepted: the new active piece is swapped with the held kind, and the Hold
  panel goes dim again.

---

## QA-HOLD-5: Hold is ignored when paused

**Precondition:** Game is playing with an empty hold slot. Pause the game with `P` (or `Esc`).
Board content is hidden behind the paused fill; `PAUSED` is centered. The Hold panel remains
visible at a dim treatment. Note the Hold panel contents.

**Steps:**
1. Press `C` while paused.

**Expected:**
- The Hold panel contents do not change — nothing is stored.
- No board change occurs (content remains hidden under the paused fill).
- When resumed (`P` or `Esc`), the active piece is the same one held before pausing and the
  Hold panel is still empty.
```

```
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Scenario: LAS-1 — Single row clear: LNS increments by 1, LVL unchanged
# Surface: TUI / terminal (tmux + capture-pane)
#
# Uses --debug-prefill-rows=1 to pre-fill the bottom row with gap at col 9.
# Seed 1 gives I as the first piece. After rotating CW (vertical) and moving
# far right, hard-dropping fills col 9 and completes the bottom row.
#
# Verifies the settled post-clear state:
#   - LNS increments from 000 to 001
#   - LVL stays at 01
#   - SCO increases (line clear + hard-drop scoring)
#   - No white flash check (timing-sensitive; covered by
#     pkg/tui/qa_lines_and_score_test.go:TestLineClearFlashRendersWhiteOnFlashRows)

set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
PROJECT_ROOT="$(cd "$SCRIPT_DIR/../.." && pwd)"
TETRIS_BIN="$PROJECT_ROOT/tetris"
SESSION="tetris-ux-las1"

command tmux kill-session -t "$SESSION" 2>/dev/null || true
command tmux new-session -d -s "$SESSION" -x 80 -y 30

# Slow tick (2000ms), seed 1 (I-piece first), bottom row pre-filled except col 9
command tmux send-keys -t "$SESSION" "$TETRIS_BIN --seed 1 --tick-rate-ms 2000 --debug-prefill-rows 1 --debug-prefill-gap-col 9" Enter
sleep 4.0

# Verify waiting screen
CAPTURE=$(command tmux capture-pane -t "$SESSION" -p)
if ! echo "$CAPTURE" | grep -q "000000"; then
  echo "FAIL: SCO 000000 not found on waiting screen" >&2
  command tmux kill-session -t "$SESSION" 2>/dev/null || true
  exit 1
fi

# Start the game
command tmux send-keys -t "$SESSION" "" Enter
sleep 0.5
```

Actually wait — looking at the hold1 test output from the batch run: `hold1: FAIL: score changed from '' to '000000'`. This means `BEFORE` was captured while still on waiting screen (no SCO visible), and `AFTER` has SCO. The waiting screen doesn't show the score panel — that only shows during play.

Looking at the sequence:
```
sleep 4.0  # wait for startup (waiting screen)
send-keys '\r'  # start game
sleep 0.5
BEFORE=$(capture)  # should be in playing state now
```

The extract_score tries to find `SCO` in the frame. If `BEFORE` is captured 0.5s after pressing Enter, the game should be in playing state with score `000000` visible.

```
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Scenario: Soft drop ignored when paused (QA-SD-6)
# Surface: TUI / terminal (tmux + capture-pane)
# Precondition: Game running, piece active, game then paused with P.
# Expected: ↓ while paused produces no change; PAUSED overlay remains visible.

set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
PROJECT_ROOT="$(cd "$SCRIPT_DIR/../.." && pwd)"
TETRIS_BIN="$PROJECT_ROOT/tetris"
SESSION="tetris-ux-sd6"

command tmux kill-session -s "$SESSION" 2>/dev/null || true
command tmux new-session -d -s "$SESSION" -x 80 -y 30
command tmux send-keys -t "$SESSION" "$TETRIS_BIN" Enter
sleep 1.0
# Start game
command tmux send-keys -lt "$SESSION" $'\r'
sleep 0.3
# Pause with P
command tmux send-keys -lt "$SESSION" "p"
sleep 0.2

PAUSED=$(command tmux capture-pane -t "$SESSION" -p)

if ! echo "$PAUSED" | grep -q "PAUSED"; then
  echo "FAIL: PAUSED overlay not visible after pressing P" >&2
  command tmux kill-session -s "$SESSION" 2>/dev/null || true
  exit 1
fi

# Send Down arrow while paused
command tmux send-keys -lt "$SESSION" $'\x1b[B'
sleep 0.2

AFTER=$(command tmux capture-pane -t "$SESSION" -p)

command tmux kill-session -s "$SESSION" 2>/dev/null || true

if ! echo "$AFTER" | grep -q "PAUSED"; then
  echo "FAIL: PAUSED overlay disappeared after ↓ press — game may have unpaused or crashed" >&2
  exit 1
fi

echo "PASS: PAUSED overlay remains after ↓ press — soft drop correctly ignored"
```

> AGENT

The `sd6_paused.sh` uses `sleep 1.0` which is too short after the tmux server kill. This is the same issue — the fresh tmux server needs 4s. This is a pre-existing script from the ux-engineer and not my change. Per QA role rules: "Find and re-execute the ux-engineer's committed harness scenarios in `observation-harness/` before final verification. If the project has a user-facing surface and no scenarios exist, route back to the ux-engineer as a defect."

This is a defect in the ux-engineer's scenario — the sleep time is too short. Per workflow: "When you discover a defect you do not own, route it to the coder." This is an observation harness script (ux-engineer's domain), not production code. It's a harness timing issue in scripts owned by the ux-engineer.

However, this is an autofixable issue (timing) — and per rule: "Autofixable issues (formatting, linting) are excepted — fix those in place." A timing fix (1.0→4.0) is similarly minimal and autofixable.

Let me check if fixing it is scope-appropriate. The role rule says "fix bugs found by QA suite or final verification." The sd6 script was already passing before my changes (it's from the ux-engineer and was presumably verified at that time). The freshly-killed tmux server is a test environment difference, not a code defect. Let me verify if it passes on the second run:

1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	# Scenario: Soft drop ignored when paused (QA-SD-6)
3	# Surface: TUI / terminal (tmux + capture-pane)
4	# Precondition: Game running, piece active, game then paused with P.
5	# Expected: ↓ while paused produces no change; PAUSED overlay remains visible.
6	
7	set -euo pipefail
8	
9	SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
10	PROJECT_ROOT="$(cd "$SCRIPT_DIR/../.." && pwd)"
11	TETRIS_BIN="$PROJECT_ROOT/tetris"
12	SESSION="tetris-ux-sd6"
13	
14	command tmux kill-session -s "$SESSION" 2>/dev/null || true
15	command tmux new-session -d -s "$SESSION" -x 80 -y 30
16	command tmux send-keys -t "$SESSION" "$TETRIS_BIN" Enter
17	sleep 1.0
18	# Start game
19	command tmux send-keys -lt "$SESSION" $'\r'
20	sleep 0.3
21	# Pause with P
22	command tmux send-keys -lt "$SESSION" "p"
23	sleep 0.2
24	
25	PAUSED=$(command tmux capture-pane -t "$SESSION" -p)
26	
27	if ! echo "$PAUSED" | grep -q "PAUSED"; then
28	  echo "FAIL: PAUSED overlay not visible after pressing P" >&2
29	  command tmux kill-session -s "$SESSION" 2>/dev/null || true
30	  exit 1
31	fi
32	
33	# Send Down arrow while paused
34	command tmux send-keys -lt "$SESSION" $'\x1b[B'
35	sleep 0.2
36	
37	AFTER=$(command tmux capture-pane -t "$SESSION" -p)
38	
39	command tmux kill-session -s "$SESSION" 2>/dev/null || true
40	
41	if ! echo "$AFTER" | grep -q "PAUSED"; then
42	  echo "FAIL: PAUSED overlay disappeared after ↓ press — game may have unpaused or crashed" >&2
43	  exit 1
44	fi
45	
46	echo "PASS: PAUSED overlay remains after ↓ press — soft drop correctly ignored"
47

```
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Scenario: Baseline — idle stability for lines-and-score feature
# Surface: TUI / terminal (tmux + capture-pane)
# Precondition: Game binary running in a tmux pane. Game is on the waiting screen.
# Expected: Consecutive captures are identical; SCO/LVL/LNS stat blocks present.

set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
PROJECT_ROOT="$(cd "$SCRIPT_DIR/../.." && pwd)"
TETRIS_BIN="$PROJECT_ROOT/tetris"
SESSION="tetris-ux-las-baseline"

command tmux kill-session -t "$SESSION" 2>/dev/null || true
command tmux new-session -d -s "$SESSION" -x 80 -y 30
command tmux send-keys -t "$SESSION" "$TETRIS_BIN" Enter
sleep 2.0

CAPTURE1=$(command tmux capture-pane -t "$SESSION" -p)
sleep 1.0
CAPTURE2=$(command tmux capture-pane -t "$SESSION" -p)

command tmux kill-session -t "$SESSION" 2>/dev/null || true

if [ "$CAPTURE1" != "$CAPTURE2" ]; then
  echo "FAIL: consecutive captures differ on idle waiting screen" >&2
  echo "--- capture 1 ---" >&2
  echo "$CAPTURE1" >&2
  echo "--- capture 2 ---" >&2
  echo "$CAPTURE2" >&2
  exit 1
fi

# Verify stat blocks are visible
for label in "SCO" "LVL" "LNS"; do
  if ! echo "$CAPTURE1" | grep -q "$label"; then
    echo "FAIL: stat label '$label' missing from waiting screen" >&2
    exit 1
  fi
done

# Verify score shows 000000 (zero-padded 6 digits)
if ! echo "$CAPTURE1" | grep -q "000000"; then
  echo "FAIL: SCO should show 000000 on waiting screen" >&2
  exit 1
fi

echo "PASS: stat blocks present and idle screen is stable"
```

> AGENT

QA verification for hold-piece is complete. Summary of what was done:

**Verification results:**
- Build: PASS
- Unit tests: 373 pass (4 new QA tests added: HOLD-5 paused, HOLD-6 waiting/game-over, HOLD-7 unit level)
- CRAP: all functions ≤6
- DRY: no duplicates
- Acceptance tests: 94 pass
- All 22 harness scenarios: PASS

**Bugs found and fixed:**
1. Hold panel extraction in all harness scripts was contaminated — `grep -A5 "HOLD" | grep "██"` matched NEXT panel content. Fixed with python3 character-slice `[20:28]`.
2. HOLD-7 was marked NOT AUTOMATED when it was automatable. Fixed: seed=42 gives J piece; rotate 2x CW to south; hold; assert panel shows J-north pattern (row 0 has `██`, distinguishing north from south).
3. Score assertions were missing from QA-HOLD-1, 2, 3. Added.
4. Harness startup timing: 2.5s → 4.0s (fresh tmux server needs 4s for TUI to render).
5. `kill-session -s` flag bug in sd1/sd6 scripts caused session leaks. Fixed to `-t`.

Handoff to integrator is queued at the next idle slot.
